*REMOVED*const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.Metal.MetalKernels.ActivationKernels = "\n#include <metal_stdlib>\n#include <metal_math>\nusing namespace metal;\n\n// Constants\nconstant float EPSILON = 1e-7f;\nconstant float PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;\nconstant float SQRT_2_OVER_PI = 0.7978845608028654f;\nconstant float GELU_COEF = 0.044715f;\n\n// Helper functions\ninline float sigmoid(float x) {\n    return 1.0f / (1.0f + exp(-x));\n}\n\ninline float safe_divide(float a, float b) {\n    return a / (b + EPSILON);\n}\n\n// IEEE-754 binary32: the bits other than the sign, and +Inf's bit pattern. A value whose magnitude bits exceed +Inf's is\n// NaN, a test the default fast math cannot fold away the way it folds isnan().\nconstant uint F32_ABS_MASK = 0x7fffffffu;\nconstant uint F32_POS_INF_BITS = 0x7f800000u;\n\ninline float clamp_value(float x, float min_val, float max_val) {\n    // torch.clamp propagates NaN.\n    return ((as_type<uint>(x) & F32_ABS_MASK) > F32_POS_INF_BITS) ? x : max(min_val, min(max_val, x));\n}\n\n\n// ReLU activation: B = max(0, A)\nkernel void relu(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        B[gid] = max(0.0f, A[gid]);\n    }\n}\n\n// ReLU backward: gradInput = gradOutput * (input > 0)\nkernel void relu_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        gradInput[gid] = input[gid] > 0.0f ? gradOutput[gid] : 0.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// Leaky ReLU: B = x > 0 ? x : alpha * x\nkernel void leaky_relu(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant float& alpha [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        B[gid] = x > 0.0f ? x : alpha * x;\n    }\n}\n\n// Leaky ReLU backward\nkernel void leaky_relu_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant float& alpha [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(4)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        gradInput[gid] = input[gid] > 0.0f ? gradOutput[gid] : alpha * gradOutput[gid];\n    }\n}\n\n// Sigmoid activation: B = 1 / (1 + exp(-A))\nkernel void sigmoid(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        // Numerically stable sigmoid\n        if (x >= 0.0f) {\n            float exp_neg_x = exp(-x);\n            B[gid] = 1.0f / (1.0f + exp_neg_x);\n        } else {\n            float exp_x = exp(x);\n            B[gid] = exp_x / (1.0f + exp_x);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Sigmoid backward: gradInput = gradOutput * sigmoid * (1 - sigmoid)\nkernel void sigmoid_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* output [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float s = output[gid];\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[gid] * s * (1.0f - s);\n    }\n}\n\n// Tanh activation\nkernel void tanh_activation(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        // Clamp to avoid NaN on some GPU drivers; tanh saturates to +/-1 for |x| > ~10\n        float x = clamp(A[gid], -20.0f, 20.0f);\n        B[gid] = tanh(x);\n    }\n}\n\n// Tanh backward: gradInput = gradOutput * (1 - tanh^2)\nkernel void tanh_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* output [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float t = output[gid];\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[gid] * (1.0f - t * t);\n    }\n}\n\n// GELU activation: x * 0.5 * (1 + tanh(sqrt(2/pi) * (x + 0.044715 * x^3)))\nkernel void gelu(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        float x3 = x * x * x;\n        float inner = SQRT_2_OVER_PI * (x + GELU_COEF * x3);\n        B[gid] = 0.5f * x * (1.0f + tanh(inner));\n    }\n}\n\n// GELU backward (approximate)\nkernel void gelu_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = input[gid];\n        float x2 = x * x;\n        float x3 = x2 * x;\n        float inner = SQRT_2_OVER_PI * (x + GELU_COEF * x3);\n        float tanh_inner = tanh(inner);\n        float sech2 = 1.0f - tanh_inner * tanh_inner;\n        float inner_deriv = SQRT_2_OVER_PI * (1.0f + 3.0f * GELU_COEF * x2);\n        float grad = 0.5f * (1.0f + tanh_inner) + 0.5f * x * sech2 * inner_deriv;\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[gid] * grad;\n    }\n}\n\n// SiLU/Swish activation: x * sigmoid(x)\nkernel void silu(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        float sig = 1.0f / (1.0f + exp(-x));\n        B[gid] = x * sig;\n    }\n}\n\n// SiLU backward\nkernel void silu_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = input[gid];\n        float sig = 1.0f / (1.0f + exp(-x));\n        float grad = sig * (1.0f + x * (1.0f - sig));\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[gid] * grad;\n    }\n}\n\n// ELU activation: x > 0 ? x : alpha * (exp(x) - 1)\nkernel void elu(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant float& alpha [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        B[gid] = x > 0.0f ? x : alpha * (exp(x) - 1.0f);\n    }\n}\n\n// SELU activation: scale * (x > 0 ? x : alpha * (exp(x) - 1))\nkernel void selu(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        const float alpha = 1.6732632423543772f;\n        const float scale = 1.0507009873554805f;\n        float x = A[gid];\n        B[gid] = scale * (x > 0.0f ? x : alpha * (exp(x) - 1.0f));\n    }\n}\n\n// Softplus: log(1 + exp(x))\nkernel void softplus(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant float& beta [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid] * beta;\n        // Numerically stable softplus\n        if (x > 20.0f) {\n            B[gid] = A[gid];\n        } else if (x < -20.0f) {\n            B[gid] = 0.0f;\n        } else {\n            B[gid] = log(1.0f + exp(x)) / beta;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Mish: x * tanh(softplus(x))\nkernel void mish(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        float sp = log(1.0f + exp(x));\n        B[gid] = x * tanh(sp);\n    }\n}\n\n// Hardswish: x * min(max(x + 3, 0), 6) / 6\nkernel void hardswish(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        float relu6 = min(max(x + 3.0f, 0.0f), 6.0f);\n        B[gid] = x * relu6 / 6.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// Hardsigmoid: min(max(x + 3, 0), 6) / 6\nkernel void hardsigmoid(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        B[gid] = min(max(x + 3.0f, 0.0f), 6.0f) / 6.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// PReLU: x > 0 ? x : weight * x (per-channel weights)\nkernel void prelu(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const (float)x * Win / Wout : 0.0;\n        int yi = (int)floor(sy); if (yi >= Hin) yi = Hin - 1;\n        int xi = (int)floor(sx); if (xi >= Win) xi = Win - 1;\n        output[gid] = src[yi * Win + xi];\n    } else if (mode == 2) {\n        float sy = source_coord_f(y, Hout, Hin, alignCorners);\n        float sx = source_coord_f(x, Wout, Win, alignCorners);\n        int y0 = (int)floor(sy); if (x0 > x1) x0 = x1; }\n        float fy = sy - y0; if (fy < 0) fy = 0; if (fy > 1) fy = 1;\n        float fx = sx - x0; if (fx < 0) fx = 0; if (fx > 1) fx = 1;\n        float v00 = src[y0 * Win + x0], v01 = src[y0 * Win + x1];\n        float v10 = src[y1 * Win + x0], v11 = src[y1 * Win + x1];\n        output[gid] = v00 * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + v01 * fx * (1 - fy)\n                    + v10 * (1 - fx) * fy + v11 * fx * fy;\n    } else if (mode == 3) {\n        float sy = source_coord_f(y, Hout, Hin, alignCorners);\n        float sx = source_coord_f(x, Wout, Win, alignCorners);\n        int y0 = (int)floor(sy); float ty = sy - y0;\n        int x0 = (int)floor(sx); float tx = sx - x0;\n        float wy[4] = { cubic_kernel_f(1.0 + ty, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(ty, -0.75),\n                        cubic_kernel_f(1.0 - ty, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(2.0 - ty, -0.75) };\n        float wx[4] = { cubic_kernel_f(1.0 + tx, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(tx, -0.75),\n                        cubic_kernel_f(1.0 - tx, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(2.0 - tx, -0.75) };\n        float acc = 0.0;\n        for (int yy = 0; yy < 4; yy++) {\n            int yi = clamp_i(y0 - 1 + yy, 0, Hin - 1);\n            float rowAcc = 0.0;\n            for (int xx = 0; xx < 4; xx++) {\n                int xi = clamp_i(x0 - 1 + xx, 0, Win - 1);\n                rowAcc += wx[xx] * src[yi * Win + xi];\n            }\n            acc += wy[yy] * rowAcc;\n        }\n        output[gid] = acc;\n    } else {  // Area — overlap-weighted averaging\n        float yLo = (float)y * Hin / Hout;\n        float yHi = (float)(y + 1) * Hin / Hout;\n        float xLo = (float)x * Win / Wout;\n        float xHi = (float)(x + 1) * Win / Wout;\n        int yL = (int)floor(yLo); int yH = (int)ceil(yHi);\n        int xL = (int)floor(xLo); int xH = (int)ceil(xHi);\n        if (yH <= yL) yH = yL + 1;\n        if (xH <= xL) xH = xL + 1;\n        if (yH > Hin) yH = Hin;\n        if (xH > Win) xH = Win;\n        float totalArea = (yHi - yLo) * (xHi - xLo);\n        float acc = 0.0;\n        for (int yy = yL; yy < yH; yy++) {\n            float oy = max(0.0f, min(yHi, (float)(yy + 1)) - max(yLo, (float)yy));\n            if (oy <= 0.0f) continue;\n            for (int xx = xL; xx < xH; xx++) {\n                float ox = max(0.0f, min(xHi, (float)(xx + 1)) - max(xLo, (float)xx));\n                if (ox <= 0.0f) continue;\n                acc += oy * ox * src[yy * Win + xx];\n            }\n        }\n        output[gid] = totalArea > 0.0 ? acc / totalArea : 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pad 4D.\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nkernel void geometry_pad_4d(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant int& N [[buffer(2)]], constant int& C [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant int& Hin [[buffer(4)]], constant int& Win [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant int& padN0 [[buffer(6)]], constant int& padN1 [[buffer(7)]],\n    constant int& padC0 [[buffer(8)]], constant int& padC1 [[buffer(9)]],\n    constant int& padH0 [[buffer(10)]], constant int& padH1 [[buffer(11)]],\n    constant int& padW0 [[buffer(12)]], constant int& padW1 [[buffer(13)]],\n    constant int& mode [[buffer(14)]], constant float& padValue [[buffer(15)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    int Nout = N + padN0 + padN1;\n    int Cout = C + padC0 + padC1;\n    int Hout = Hin + padH0 + padH1;\n    int Wout = Win + padW0 + padW1;\n    int total = Nout * Cout * Hout * Wout;\n    if ((int)gid >= total) return;\n    int w = (int)gid % Wout; int t1 = (int)gid / Wout;\n    int h = t1 % Hout; int t2 = t1 / Hout;\n    int c = t2 % Cout; int n = t2 / Cout;\n    int nn = n - padN0, cc = c - padC0, hh = h - padH0, ww = w - padW0;\n    bool inB = nn >= 0 && nn < N && cc >= 0 && cc < C && hh >= 0 && hh < Hin && ww >= 0 && ww < Win;\n    if (!inB && mode == 0) { output[gid] = padValue; return; }\n    if (!inB) {\n        if (!(nn >= 0 && nn < N)) nn = pad_boundary(nn, N, mode);\n        if (!(cc >= 0 && cc < C)) cc = pad_boundary(cc, C, mode);\n        if (!(hh >= 0 && hh < Hin)) hh = pad_boundary(hh, Hin, mode);\n        if (!(ww >= 0 && ww < Win)) ww = pad_boundary(ww, Win, mode);\n    }\n    output[gid] = input[((nn * C + cc) * Hin + hh) * Win + ww];\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// GridSample 2D, NCHW input/output.\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninline float grid_sample_safe_msl(device const float* src, int n, int y, int x, int c,\n    int H, int W, int C, int padding)\n{\n    if (padding == 0) {\n        if ((uint)y >= (uint)H || (uint)x >= (uint)W) return 0.0;\n    } else if (padding == 1) {\n        y = clamp_i(y, 0, H - 1); x = clamp_i(x, 0, W - 1);\n    } else {\n        y = reflect_index(y, H); x = reflect_index(x, W);\n    }\n    return src[((n * C + c) * H + y) * W + x];\n}\n\nkernel void geometry_grid_sample_2d(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* grid [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant int& N [[buffer(3)]], constant int& H [[buffer(4)]],\n    constant int& W [[buffer(5)]], constant int& C [[buffer(6)]],\n    constant int& outH [[buffer(7)]], constant int& outW [[buffer(8)]],\n    constant int& mode [[buffer(9)]], constant int& padding [[buffer(10)]],\n    constant int& alignCorners [[buffer(11)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    int total = N * outH * outW;\n    if ((int)gid >= total) return;\n    int ox = (int)gid % outW; int t1 = (int)gid / outW;\n    int oy = t1 % outH; int n = t1 / outH;\n    int gOff = ((n * outH + oy) * outW + ox) * 2;\n    float gx = grid[gOff];\n    float gy = grid[gOff + 1];\n    float sx = alignCorners ? (gx + 1.0) * 0.5 * (W - 1) : ((gx + 1.0) * W - 1.0) * 0.5;\n    float sy = alignCorners ? (gy + 1.0) * 0.5 * (H - 1) : ((gy + 1.0) * H - 1.0) * 0.5;\n    // #1134: a NaN, infinite or out-of-int-range point samples nothing (the CPU rule). Tested on the bits, so fast\n    // math cannot fold the NaN case away: |x| below 2147483520 has a smaller bit pattern, NaN and infinity a larger one.\n    const uint limBits = 0x4EFFFFFFu; // 2147483520.0f, the largest float below 2^31\n    if ((as_type<uint>(sx) & 0x7FFFFFFFu) >= limBits || (as_type<uint>(sy) & 0x7FFFFFFFu) >= limBits) {\n        for (int c = 0; c < C; c++) output[((n * C + c) * outH + oy) * outW + ox] = 0.0f;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    if (mode == 1) {\n        int nx = (int)rint(sx), ny = (int)rint(sy);\n        for (int c = 0; c < C; c++)\n            output[((n * C + c) * outH + oy) * outW + ox] =\n                grid_sample_safe_msl(input, n, ny, nx, c, H, W, C, padding);\n    } else if (mode == 0) {\n        int x0 = (int)floor(sx), y0 = (int)floor(sy);\n        int x1 = x0 + 1, y1 = y0 + 1;\n        float fx = sx - x0, fy = sy - y0;\n        for (int c = 0; c < C; c++) {\n            float v00 = grid_sample_safe_msl(input, n, y0, x0, c, H, W, C, padding);\n            float v01 = grid_sample_safe_msl(input, n, y0, x1, c, H, W, C, padding);\n            float v10 = grid_sample_safe_msl(input, n, y1, x0, c, H, W, C, padding);\n            float v11 = grid_sample_safe_msl(input, n, y1, x1, c, H, W, C, padding);\n            output[((n * C + c) * outH + oy) * outW + ox] =\n                v00 * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + v01 * fx * (1 - fy)\n              + v10 * (1 - fx) * fy + v11 * fx * fy;\n        }\n    } else {\n        int x0 = (int)floor(sx), y0 = (int)floor(sy);\n        float fx = sx - x0, fy = sy - y0;\n        float wy[4] = { cubic_kernel_f(1.0 + fy, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(fy, -0.75),\n                        cubic_kernel_f(1.0 - fy, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(2.0 - fy, -0.75) };\n        float wx[4] = { cubic_kernel_f(1.0 + fx, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(fx, -0.75),\n                        cubic_kernel_f(1.0 - fx, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(2.0 - fx, -0.75) };\n        for (int c = 0; c < C; c++) {\n            float acc = 0.0;\n            for (int yy = 0; yy < 4; yy++) {\n                int yi = y0 - 1 + yy;\n                float rowAcc = 0.0;\n                for (int xx = 0; xx < 4; xx++) {\n                    int xi = x0 - 1 + xx;\n                    rowAcc += wx[xx] * grid_sample_safe_msl(input, n, yi, xi, c, H, W, C, padding);\n                }\n                acc += wy[yy] * rowAcc;\n            }\n            output[((n * C + c) * outH + oy) * outW + ox] = acc;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// AffineGrid 3D.\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninline float grid_norm_coord_f(int idx, int size, int alignCorners) {\n    if (size <= 1) return 0.0;\n    return alignCorners ? -1.0 + 2.0 * idx / (size - 1) : -1.0 + (2.0 * idx + 1.0) / size;\n}\n\nkernel void geometry_affine_grid_3d(\n    device const float* theta [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* grid [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant int& N [[buffer(2)]], constant int& D [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant int& H [[buffer(4)]], constant int& W [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant int& alignCorners [[buffer(6)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    int total = N * D * H * W;\n    if ((int)gid >= total) return;\n    int w = (int)gid % W; int t1 = (int)gid / W;\n    int h = t1 % H; int t2 = t1 / H;\n    int d = t2 % D; int n = t2 / D;\n    int tBase = n * 12;\n    float x = grid_norm_coord_f(w, W, alignCorners);\n    float y = grid_norm_coord_f(h, H, alignCorners);\n    float z = grid_norm_coord_f(d, D, alignCorners);\n    int gBase = (((n * D + d) * H + h) * W + w) * 3;\n    for (int row = 0; row < 3; row++) {\n        grid[gBase + row] = theta[tBase + row * 4] * x\n                          + theta[tBase + row * 4 + 1] * y\n                          + theta[tBase + row * 4 + 2] * z\n                          + theta[tBase + row * 4 + 3];\n    }\n}\n" -> string!

AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.IGeometryBackwardBackend
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.Metal.MetalGeometryKernels.Source = "\n#include <metal_stdlib>\n#include <metal_math>\nusing namespace metal;\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Shared device helpers.\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninline float source_coord_f(int dstIdx, int dstSize, int srcSize, int alignCorners) {\n    if (dstSize <= 1) return 0.0;\n    if (alignCorners) return float(dstIdx) * (srcSize - 1) / (dstSize - 1);\n    return (float(dstIdx) + 0.5) * srcSize / dstSize - 0.5;\n}\n\ninline float cubic_kernel_f(float d, float a) {\n    float ad = fabs(d);\n    if (ad < 1.0) return ((a + 2.0) * ad - (a + 3.0)) * ad * ad + 1.0;\n    if (ad < 2.0) return a * ((ad - 5.0) * ad + 8.0) * ad - 4.0 * a;\n    return 0.0;\n}\n\ninline int clamp_i(int v, int lo, int hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }\n\ninline int reflect_index(int i, int extent) {\n    if (extent == 1) return 0;\n    int period = 2 * (extent - 1);\n    int r = ((i % period) + period) % period;\n    return r < extent ? r : period - r;\n}\n\ninline int pad_boundary(int idx, int extent, int mode) {\n    if (extent <= 0) return 0;\n    if (mode == 2) { if (idx < 0) return 0; if (idx >= extent) return extent - 1; return idx; }\n    if (mode == 1) return reflect_index(idx, extent);\n    int r = ((idx % extent) + extent) % extent;\n    return r;\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Interpolate 2D.\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nkernel void geometry_interpolate_2d(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant int& N [[buffer(2)]], constant int& C [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant int& Hin [[buffer(4)]], constant int& Win [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant int& Hout [[buffer(6)]], constant int& Wout [[buffer(7)]],\n    constant int& mode [[buffer(8)]], constant int& alignCorners [[buffer(9)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    int total = N * C * Hout * Wout;\n    if ((int)gid >= total) return;\n    int x = (int)gid % Wout; int t1 = (int)gid / Wout;\n    int y = t1 % Hout; int t2 = t1 / Hout;\n    int c = t2 % C; int n = t2 / C;\n    device const float* src = input + ((n * C + c) * Hin) * Win;\n\n    if (mode == 0) {\n        // floor(x * Win / Wout) in integers: a float division here is not correctly rounded (drivers may compute\n        // a * (1 / b)), so an exact quotient such as 30 / 15 came out as 1.99999994 and floored to the wrong pixel.\n        int yi = (y * Hin) / Hout; if (yi >= Hin) yi = Hin - 1;\n        int xi = (x * Win) / Wout; if (xi >= Win) xi = Win - 1;\n        output[gid] = src[yi * Win + xi];\n    } else if (mode == 2) {\n        float sy = source_coord_f(y, Hout, Hin, alignCorners);\n        float sx = source_coord_f(x, Wout, Win, alignCorners);\n        int y0 = (int)floor(sy); int x0 = (int)floor(sx);\n        if (y0 < 0) y0 = 0; if (x0 < 0) x0 = 0;\n        int y1 = y0 + 1; int x1 = x0 + 1;\n        if (y1 >= Hin) { y1 = Hin - 1; if (y0 > y1) y0 = y1; }\n        if (x1 >= Win) { x1 = Win - 1; if (x0 > x1) x0 = x1; }\n        float fy = sy - y0; if (fy < 0) fy = 0; if (fy > 1) fy = 1;\n        float fx = sx - x0; if (fx < 0) fx = 0; if (fx > 1) fx = 1;\n        float v00 = src[y0 * Win + x0], v01 = src[y0 * Win + x1];\n        float v10 = src[y1 * Win + x0], v11 = src[y1 * Win + x1];\n        output[gid] = v00 * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + v01 * fx * (1 - fy)\n                    + v10 * (1 - fx) * fy + v11 * fx * fy;\n    } else if (mode == 3) {\n        float sy = source_coord_f(y, Hout, Hin, alignCorners);\n        float sx = source_coord_f(x, Wout, Win, alignCorners);\n        int y0 = (int)floor(sy); float ty = sy - y0;\n        int x0 = (int)floor(sx); float tx = sx - x0;\n        float wy[4] = { cubic_kernel_f(1.0 + ty, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(ty, -0.75),\n                        cubic_kernel_f(1.0 - ty, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(2.0 - ty, -0.75) };\n        float wx[4] = { cubic_kernel_f(1.0 + tx, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(tx, -0.75),\n                        cubic_kernel_f(1.0 - tx, -0.75), cubic_kernel_f(2.0 - tx, -0.75) };\n        float acc = 0.0;\n        for (int yy = 0; yy < 4; yy++) {\n            int yi = clamp_i(y0 - 1 + yy, 0, Hin - 1);\n            float rowAcc = 0.0;\n            for (int xx = 0; xx < 4; xx++) {\n                int xi = clamp_i(x0 - 1 + xx, 0, Win - 1);\n                rowAcc += wx[xx] * src[yi * Win + xi];\n            }\n            acc += wy[yy] * rowAcc;\n        }\n        output[gid] = acc;\n    } else {  // Area — overlap-weighted averaging\n        // Positions in units of 1/Hout (1/Wout): output cell y covers [y * Hin, (y + 1) * Hin) and input pixel yy\n        // covers [yy * Hout, (yy + 1) * Hout), so every bound and overlap is an exact integer; a float division is\n        // not correctly rounded here and could open the window a pixel wide with a spurious sliver of weight.\n        int yLo = y * Hin, yHi = (y + 1) * Hin;\n        int xLo = x * Win, xHi = (x + 1) * Win;\n        int yL = yLo / Hout, yH = min((yHi + Hout - 1) / Hout, Hin);\n        int xL = xLo / Wout, xH = min((xHi + Wout - 1) / Wout, Win);\n        float acc = 0.0f;\n        for (int yy = yL; yy < yH; yy++) {\n            int oy = min(yHi, (yy + 1) * Hout) - max(yLo, yy * Hout);\n            if (oy <= 0) continue;\n            for (int xx = xL; xx < xH; xx++) {\n                int ox = min(xHi, (xx + 1) * Wout) - max(xLo, xx * Wout);\n                if (ox <= 0) continue;\n                acc += (float)(oy * ox) * src[yy * Win + xx];\n            }\n        }\n        output[gid] = acc / (float)(Hin * Win);\n    }\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pad 4D.\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nkernel void geometry_pad_4d(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant int& N [[buffer(2)]], constant int& C [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant int& Hin [[buffer(4)]], constant int& Win [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant int& padN0 [[buffer(6)]], constant int& padN1 [[buffer(7)]],\n    constant int& padC0 [[buffer(8)]], constant int& padC1 [[buffer(9)]],\n    constant int& padH0 [[buffer(10)]], constant int& padH1 [[buffer(11)]],\n    constant int& padW0 [[buffer(12)]], constant int& padW1 [[buffer(13)]],\n    constant int& mode [[buffer(14)]], constant float& padValue [[buffer(15)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    int Nout = N + padN0 + padN1;\n    int Cout = C + padC0 + padC1;\n    int Hout = Hin + padH0 + padH1;\n    int Wout = Win + padW0 + padW1;\n    int total = Nout * Cout * Hout * Wout;\n    if ((int)gid >= total) return;\n    int w = (int)gid % Wout; int t1 = (int)gid / Wout;\n    int h = t1 % Hout; int t2 = t1 / Hout;\n    int c = t2 % Cout; int n = t2 / Cout;\n    int nn = n - padN0, cc = c - padC0, hh = h - padH0, ww = w - padW0;\n    bool inB = nn >= 0 && nn < N && cc >= 0 && cc < C && hh >= 0 && hh < Hin && ww >= 0 && ww < Win;\n    if (!inB && mode == 0) { output[gid] = padValue; return; }\n    if (!inB) {\n        if (!(nn >= 0 && nn < N)) nn = pad_boundary(nn, N, mode);\n        if (!(cc >= 0 && cc < C)) cc = pad_boundary(cc, C, mode);\n        if (!(hh >= 0 && hh < Hin)) hh = pad_boundary(hh, Hin, mode);\n        if (!(ww >= 0 && ww < Win)) ww = pad_boundary(ww, Win, mode);\n    }\n    output[gid] = input[((nn * C + cc) * Hin + hh) * Win + ww];\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// GridSample 2D, NCHW input/output.\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// GridSample 2D forward and backward, every mode and padding (#1680), with PyTorch's grid_sampler_2d\n// padding rules. One tap helper gives each point's pixels, weights and weight derivatives; the forward sums\n// over them and the backward differentiates the same sum. One thread per sampling point, looping C.\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// PyTorch's grid_sampler_compute_source_index for Border (1) and Reflection (2), with *slope = d(result)/d(s):\n// Reflection reflects about -0.5 and S-0.5 (0 and S-1 with alignCorners), then both clip to [0, S-1]. A clipped\n// coordinate has slope 0. Bilinear and Nearest pad the coordinate this way; Bicubic pads each tap instead.\ninline float grid_sample_pad_coord(float s, int size, int padding, int alignCorners, thread float* slope)\n{\n    *slope = 1.0;\n    if (padding == 0) return s;\n    if (padding == 2) {\n        if (size == 1 && alignCorners) { *slope = 0.0; return 0.0; }\n        float twiceLow = alignCorners ? 0.0 : -1.0;\n        float twiceHigh = alignCorners ? 2.0 * (size - 1) : 2.0 * size - 1.0;\n        float mn = twiceLow * 0.5, span = (twiceHigh - twiceLow) * 0.5;\n        float x = s - mn;\n        if (x < 0.0) { *slope = -1.0; x = -x; }\n        float extra = fmod(x, span);\n        float flips = floor(x / span);\n        if (fmod(flips, (float)2.0) == 0.0) s = extra + mn;\n        else { s = span - extra + mn; *slope = -*slope; }\n    }\n    if (s <= 0.0) { *slope = 0.0; return 0.0; }\n    if (s >= size - 1) { *slope = 0.0; return (float)(size - 1); }\n    return s;\n}\n\ninline int grid_sample_in_bounds(int i, int extent)\n{\n    return (uint)i < (uint)extent ? i : -1;\n}\n\n// Bicubic's padded tap (PyTorch's compute_coordinates on an integer tap): without alignCorners Reflection\n// mirrors about -0.5 and S-0.5, repeating the edge pixel (period 2S); with it, about 0 and S-1.\ninline int grid_sample_bicubic_tap(int i, int extent, int padding, int alignCorners)\n{\n    if (padding == 0) return grid_sample_in_bounds(i, extent);\n    if (padding == 1) return clamp_i(i, 0, extent - 1);\n    if (alignCorners) return reflect_index(i, extent);\n    int period = 2 * extent;\n    int r = ((i % period) + period) % period;\n    return r < extent ? r : period - 1 - r;\n}\n\n// dK/dd of cubic_kernel_f for d >= 0, which every offset the taps use is.\ninline float cubic_kernel_deriv_f(float d, float a)\n{\n    if (d < 1.0) return (3.0 * (a + 2.0) * d - 2.0 * (a + 3.0)) * d;\n    if (d < 2.0) return (3.0 * a * d - 10.0 * a) * d + 8.0 * a;\n    return 0.0;\n}\n\n// The taps of one sampling point: per-axis pixel index, weight and d(weight)/d(source coord). Returns the taps\n// per axis (1 nearest, 2 bilinear, 4 bicubic), or 0 when the forward samples nothing at this point (#1134).\ninline int grid_sample_taps(device const float* grid, int gOff, int H, int W,\n    int mode, int padding, int alignCorners,\n    thread int* tx, thread int* ty, thread float* wx, thread float* wy, thread float* dwx, thread float* dwy)\n{\n    float gx = grid[gOff];\n    float gy = grid[gOff + 1];\n    float sx = alignCorners ? (gx + 1.0) * 0.5 * (W - 1) : ((gx + 1.0) * W - 1.0) * 0.5;\n    float sy = alignCorners ? (gy + 1.0) * 0.5 * (H - 1) : ((gy + 1.0) * H - 1.0) * 0.5;\n    const uint limBits = 0x4EFFFFFFu; // 2147483520.0f, the largest float below 2^31\n    if ((as_type<uint>(sx) & 0x7FFFFFFFu) >= limBits || (as_type<uint>(sy) & 0x7FFFFFFFu) >= limBits) return 0;\n    if (mode != 2) {\n        float slx, sly;\n        float px = grid_sample_pad_coord(sx, W, padding, alignCorners, &slx);\n        float py = grid_sample_pad_coord(sy, H, padding, alignCorners, &sly);\n        if (mode == 1) {\n            // rint rounds half to even, as PyTorch's nearbyint.\n            tx[0] = grid_sample_in_bounds((int)rint(px), W);\n            ty[0] = grid_sample_in_bounds((int)rint(py), H);\n            wx[0] = 1.0; wy[0] = 1.0; dwx[0] = 0.0; dwy[0] = 0.0;\n            return 1;\n        }\n        int px0 = (int)floor(px), py0 = (int)floor(py);\n        float fx = px - px0, fy = py - py0;\n        wx[0] = 1.0f - fx; wx[1] = fx; dwx[0] = -slx; dwx[1] = slx;\n        wy[0] = 1.0f - fy; wy[1] = fy; dwy[0] = -sly; dwy[1] = sly;\n        for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) {\n            tx[k] = grid_sample_in_bounds(px0 + k, W);\n            ty[k] = grid_sample_in_bounds(py0 + k, H);\n        }\n        return 2;\n    }\n    int x0 = (int)floor(sx), y0 = (int)floor(sy);\n    float fx = sx - x0, fy = sy - y0;\n    const float a = -0.75;\n    wx[0] = cubic_kernel_f(1.0 + fx, a); wx[1] = cubic_kernel_f(fx, a);\n    wx[2] = cubic_kernel_f(1.0 - fx, a); wx[3] = cubic_kernel_f(2.0 - fx, a);\n    wy[0] = cubic_kernel_f(1.0 + fy, a); wy[1] = cubic_kernel_f(fy, a);\n    wy[2] = cubic_kernel_f(1.0 - fy, a); wy[3] = cubic_kernel_f(2.0 - fy, a);\n    // Offsets 1+t and t grow with t, 1-t and 2-t shrink: hence the sign flip on the last two.\n    dwx[0] = cubic_kernel_deriv_f(1.0 + fx, a); dwx[1] = cubic_kernel_deriv_f(fx, a);\n    dwx[2] = -cubic_kernel_deriv_f(1.0 - fx, a); k < 4; k++) {\n        tx[k] = grid_sample_bicubic_tap(x0 - 1 + k, W, padding, alignCorners);\n        ty[k] = grid_sample_bicubic_tap(y0 - 1 + k, H, padding, alignCorners);\n    }\n    return 4;\n}\n\nkernel void geometry_grid_sample_2d(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* grid [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant int& N [[buffer(3)]], constant int& H [[buffer(4)]],\n    constant int& W [[buffer(5)]], constant int& C [[buffer(6)]],\n    constant int& outH [[buffer(7)]], constant int& outW [[buffer(8)]],\n    constant int& mode [[buffer(9)]], constant int& padding [[buffer(10)]],\n    constant int& alignCorners [[buffer(11)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    int total = N * outH * outW;\n    if ((int)gid >= total) return;\n    int ox = (int)gid % outW; (gy + 1.0) * 0.5 * (H - 1) : ((gy + 1.0) * H - 1.0) * 0.5;\n    // #1134: a NaN, infinite or out-of-int-range point samples nothing (the CPU rule). Tested on the bits, so fast\n    // math cannot fold the NaN case away: |x| below 2147483520 has a smaller bit pattern, NaN and infinity a larger one.\n    const uint limBits = 0x4EFFFFFFu; // 2147483520.0f, the largest float below 2^31\n    if ((as_type<uint>(sx) & 0x7FFFFFFFu) >= limBits || (as_type<uint>(sy) & 0x7FFFFFFFu) >= limBits) {\n        for (int c = 0; (H - 1) * 0.5 : H * 0.5);\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// AffineGrid 3D.\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninline float grid_norm_coord_f(int idx, int size, int alignCorners) {\n    if (size <= 1) return 0.0;\n    return alignCorners ? -1.0 + 2.0 * idx / (size - 1) : -1.0 + (2.0 * idx + 1.0) / size;\n}\n\nkernel void geometry_affine_grid_3d(\n    device const float* theta [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* grid [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant int& N [[buffer(2)]], constant int& D [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant int& H [[buffer(4)]], constant int& W [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant int& alignCorners [[buffer(6)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    int total = N * D * H * W;\n    if ((int)gid >= total) return;\n    int w = (int)gid % W; int t1 = (int)gid / W;\n    int h = t1 % H; int t2 = t1 / H;\n    int d = t2 % D; int n = t2 / D;\n    int tBase = n * 12;\n    float x = grid_norm_coord_f(w, W, alignCorners);\n    float y = grid_norm_coord_f(h, H, alignCorners);\n    float z = grid_norm_coord_f(d, D, alignCorners);\n    int gBase = (((n * D + d) * H + h) * W + w) * 3;\n    for (int row = 0; row < 3; row++) {\n        grid[gBase + row] = theta[tBase + row * 4] * x\n                          + theta[tBase + row * 4 + 1] * y\n                          + theta[tBase + row * 4 + 2] * z\n                          + theta[tBase + row * 4 + 3];\n    }\n}\n" -> string!
const AiDotNet.Tensors.Engines.DirectGpu.Metal.MetalKernels.ActivationKernels = "\n#include <metal_stdlib>\n#include <metal_math>\nusing namespace metal;\n\n// Constants\nconstant float EPSILON = 1e-7f;\nconstant float PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;\nconstant float SQRT_2_OVER_PI = 0.7978845608028654f;\nconstant float GELU_COEF = 0.044715f;\n\n// Helper functions\ninline float sigmoid(float x) {\n    return 1.0f / (1.0f + exp(-x));\n}\n\ninline float safe_divide(float a, float b) {\n    return a / (b + EPSILON);\n}\n\n// IEEE-754 binary32: the bits other than the sign, and +Inf's bit pattern. A value whose magnitude bits exceed +Inf's is\n// NaN, a test the default fast math cannot fold away the way it folds isnan().\nconstant uint F32_ABS_MASK = 0x7fffffffu;\nconstant uint F32_POS_INF_BITS = 0x7f800000u;\n\ninline float clamp_value(float x, float min_val, float max_val) {\n    // torch.clamp propagates NaN.\n    return ((as_type<uint>(x) & F32_ABS_MASK) > F32_POS_INF_BITS) ? x : max(min_val, min(max_val, x));\n}\n\n#ifndef AIDN_EXACT_FLOAT_HELPERS\n#define AIDN_EXACT_FLOAT_HELPERS\ninline bool xf_isnan(uint b) { return (b & 0x7FFFFFFFu) > 0x7F800000u; }\ninline int xf_key(uint b) { int m = int(b & 0x7FFFFFFFu); return (b & 0x80000000u) != 0u ? -m : m; }\ninline bool xf_gt(uint a, uint b) { return !xf_isnan(a) && !xf_isnan(b) && xf_key(a) > xf_key(b); }\ninline bool xf_lt(uint a, uint b) { return xf_gt(b, a); }\ninline bool xf_eq(uint a, uint b) { return !xf_isnan(a) && !xf_isnan(b) && xf_key(a) == xf_key(b); }\ninline uint xf_max(uint xf_gt(c, hi) ? hi : c; }\ninline uint xf_sign(uint x) { return xf_isnan(x) ? 0u : (xf_key(x) > 0 ? 0x3F800000u : (xf_key(x) < 0 ? 0xBF800000u : 0u)); }\ninline bool xf_truthy(uint c) { return (c & as_type<uint>(a) : as_type<uint>(b)); }\n#endif\n\n\n// ReLU activation: B = max(0, A)\nkernel void relu(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        // Numerically stable sigmoid\n        if (x >= 0.0f) {\n            float exp_neg_x = exp(-x);\n            B[gid] = 1.0f / (1.0f + exp_neg_x);\n        } else {\n            float exp_x = exp(x);\n            B[gid] = exp_x / (1.0f + exp_x);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Sigmoid backward: gradInput = gradOutput * sigmoid * (1 - sigmoid)\nkernel void sigmoid_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* output [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float s = output[gid];\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[gid] * s * (1.0f - s);\n    }\n}\n\n// Tanh activation\nkernel void tanh_activation(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        // Clamp to avoid NaN on some GPU drivers; tanh saturates to +/-1 for |x| > ~10\n        float x = clamp(A[gid], -20.0f, 20.0f);\n        B[gid] = tanh(x);\n    }\n}\n\n// Tanh backward: gradInput = gradOutput * (1 - tanh^2)\nkernel void tanh_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* output [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float t = output[gid];\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[gid] * (1.0f - t * t);\n    }\n}\n\n// GELU activation: x * 0.5 * (1 + tanh(sqrt(2/pi) * (x + 0.044715 * x^3)))\nkernel void gelu(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        float x3 = x * x * x;\n        float inner = SQRT_2_OVER_PI * (x + GELU_COEF * x3);\n        B[gid] = 0.5f * x * (1.0f + tanh(inner));\n    }\n}\n\n// GELU backward (approximate)\nkernel void gelu_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = input[gid];\n        float x2 = x * x;\n        float x3 = x2 * x;\n        float inner = SQRT_2_OVER_PI * (x + GELU_COEF * x3);\n        float tanh_inner = tanh(inner);\n        float sech2 = 1.0f - tanh_inner * tanh_inner;\n        float inner_deriv = SQRT_2_OVER_PI * (1.0f + 3.0f * GELU_COEF * x2);\n        float grad = 0.5f * (1.0f + tanh_inner) + 0.5f * x * sech2 * inner_deriv;\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[gid] * grad;\n    }\n}\n\n// SiLU/Swish activation: x * sigmoid(x)\nkernel void silu(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        float sig = 1.0f / (1.0f + exp(-x));\n        B[gid] = x * sig;\n    }\n}\n\n// SiLU backward\nkernel void silu_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = input[gid];\n        float sig = 1.0f / (1.0f + exp(-x));\n        float grad = sig * (1.0f + x * (1.0f - sig));\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[gid] * grad;\n    }\n}\n\n// ELU activation: x > 0 ? x : alpha * (exp(x) - 1)\nkernel void elu(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant float& alpha [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        B[gid] = x > 0.0f ? x : alpha * (exp(x) - 1.0f);\n    }\n}\n\n// SELU activation: scale * (x > 0 ? x : alpha * (exp(x) - 1))\nkernel void selu(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        const float alpha = 1.6732632423543772f;\n        const float scale = 1.0507009873554805f;\n        float x = A[gid];\n        B[gid] = scale * (x > 0.0f ? x : alpha * (exp(x) - 1.0f));\n    }\n}\n\n// Softplus: log(1 + exp(x))\nkernel void softplus(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant float& beta [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid] * beta;\n        // Numerically stable softplus\n        if (x > 20.0f) {\n            B[gid] = A[gid];\n        } else if (x < -20.0f) {\n            B[gid] = 0.0f;\n        } else {\n            B[gid] = log(1.0f + exp(x)) / beta;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Mish: x * tanh(softplus(x))\nkernel void mish(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        float sp = log(1.0f + exp(x));\n        B[gid] = x * tanh(sp);\n    }\n}\n\n// Hardswish: x * min(max(x + 3, 0), 6) / 6\nkernel void hardswish(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        float relu6 = min(max(x + 3.0f, 0.0f), 6.0f);\n        B[gid] = x * relu6 / 6.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// Hardsigmoid: min(max(x + 3, 0), 6) / 6\nkernel void hardsigmoid(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = A[gid];\n        B[gid] = min(max(x + 3.0f, 0.0f), 6.0f) / 6.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// PReLU: x > 0 ? x : weight * x (per-channel weights)\nkernel void prelu(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* weight [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& channels [[buffer(4)]],\n    constant uint& spatial_size [[buffer(5)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        uint channel = (gid / spatial_size) % channels;\n        float x = input[gid];\n        float w = weight[channel];\n        output[gid] = x > 0.0f ? x : w * x;\n    }\n}\n\n// ReLU6: min(max(0, x), 6)\nkernel void relu6(\n    device const float* A [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* B [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(2)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        B[gid] = xf_clampf(A[gid], 0.0f, 6.0f);   // the CPU clamp(x, 0, 6) on the bits: -0 and NaN pass through\n    }\n}\n\nkernel void relu6_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = input[gid];\n        gradInput[gid] = (x > 0.0f && x < 6.0f) ? gradOutput[gid] : 0.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// RReLU: x >= 0 ? x : noise * x\nkernel void rrelu(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* noise [[buffer(1)]],\n [[buffer(2)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(4)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = input[gid];\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[gid] * (x >= 0.0f ? 1.0f : noise[gid]);\n    }\n}\n\n// Threshold: x > threshold ? x : value\nkernel void threshold_forward(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant float& thresh [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant float& val [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(4)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        output[gid] = input[gid] > thresh ? input[gid] : val;\n    }\n}\n\nkernel void threshold_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant float& thresh [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(4)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        gradInput[gid] = input[gid] > thresh ? gradOutput[gid] : 0.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// Reciprocal backward: -grad / (x * x)\nkernel void reciprocal_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        // The second buffer is the reciprocal OUTPUT y = 1/x, so d(1/x)/dx = -1/x^2 = -y^2 (#775, #1680).\n        float y = input[gid];\n        // g * y first: y * y overflows float for |y| > 1.8e19 even when the derivative g * y^2 does not.\n        gradInput[gid] = -(gradOutput[gid] * y) * y;\n    }\n}\n\n// Variance backward: dx = gradOutput * 2*(x - mean)/n\nkernel void var_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device const float* mean [[buffer(2)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& outerSize [[buffer(4)]],\n    constant uint& reduceSize [[buffer(5)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    uint total = outerSize * reduceSize;\n    if (gid < total) {\n        uint outer = gid / reduceSize;\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[outer] * 2.0f * (input[gid] - mean[outer]) / float(reduceSize);\n    }\n}\n\n// Std backward: dx = gradOutput * (x - mean) / (n * std)\nkernel void std_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device const float* mean [[buffer(2)]],\n    device const float* stddev [[buffer(3)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(4)]],\n    constant uint& outerSize [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant uint& reduceSize [[buffer(6)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    uint total = outerSize * reduceSize;\n    if (gid < total) {\n        uint }\n}\n\n// Where backward: route gradient based on condition\nkernel void where_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* condition [[buffer(1)]],\n reduceSize;\n    if (gid < total) {\n        uint outer = gid / reduceSize;\n        float n = max(norm[outer], 1e-8f);\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[outer] * input[gid] / n;\n    }\n}\n\n// LogSumExp backward: dx = gradOutput * softmax(x)\nkernel void logsumexp_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device const [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    uint total = outerSize * reduceSize;\n    if (gid < total) {\n        uint outer = gid / reduceSize;\n        float softmax_val = exp(input[gid] - lse[outer]);\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[outer] * softmax_val;\n    }\n}\n\n// 1D Average Pooling\nkernel void avg_pool1d(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& batch [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& channels [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& inLength [[buffer(4)]],\n    constant uint& outLength [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant uint& kernelSize [[buffer(6)]],\n    constant uint& stride [[buffer(7)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    uint total = batch * channels * outLength;\n    if (gid < total) {\n        uint o = gid % outLength; uint c = (gid / outLength) % channels; uint b = gid / (outLength * channels);\n        uint inOff = (b * channels + c) * inLength;\n        float sum = 0.0f; uint cnt = 0;\n        for (uint k = 0; k < kernelSize; k++) { uint pos = o * stride + k; if (pos < inLength) { sum += input[inOff + pos]; cnt++; } }\n        output[gid] = cnt > 0 ? sum / float(cnt) : 0.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// 1D Max Pooling\nkernel void max_pool1d(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& batch [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& channels [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& inLength [[buffer(4)]],\n    constant uint& outLength [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant uint& kernelSize [[buffer(6)]],\n    constant uint& stride [[buffer(7)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    uint total = batch * channels * outLength;\n    if (gid < total) {\n        uint o = gid % outLength; uint c = (gid / outLength) % channels; uint b = gid / (outLength * channels);\n        uint inOff = (b * channels + c) * inLength;\n        // The CPU max-pool rule on the bits: the window's first cell, then only a strictly greater one.\n        uint maxBits = 0xFF800000u; bool have = false;\n        for (uint k = 0; k < kernelSize; k++) {\n            uint pos = o * stride + k;\n            if (pos < inLength) { uint v = as_type<uint>(input[inOff + pos]); if (!have || xf_gt(v, maxBits)) { maxBits = v; have = true; } }\n        }\n        output[gid] = as_type<float>(maxBits);\n    }\n}\n\n// Bilinear Upsample 2D\nkernel void bilinear_upsample2d(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& batch [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& channels [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& inH [[buffer(4)]],\n    constant uint& inW [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant uint& outH [[buffer(6)]],\n    constant uint& outW [[buffer(7)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    uint total = batch * channels * outH * outW;\n    if (gid < total) {\n        uint ow = gid % outW; uint oh = (gid / outW) % outH; uint c = (gid / (outW * outH)) % channels; uint b = gid / (outW * outH * channels);\n        float h_ratio = (outH > 1) ? float(inH - 1) / float(outH - 1) : 0.0f;\n        float w_ratio = (outW > 1) ? float(inW - 1) / float(outW - 1) : 0.0f;\n        float h_in = float(oh) * h_ratio; float w_in = float(ow) * w_ratio;\n        uint h0 = uint(h_in); uint h1 = min(h0 + 1, inH - 1); uint w0 = uint(w_in); uint w1 = min(w0 + 1, inW - 1);\n        float hd = h_in - float(h0); float wd = w_in - float(w0);\n        uint base_idx = (b * channels + c) * inH * inW;\n        output[gid] = (1-hd)*(1-wd)*input[base_idx+h0*inW+w0] + (1-hd)*wd*input[base_idx+h0*inW+w1] + hd*(1-wd)*input[base_idx+h1*inW+w0] + hd*wd*input[base_idx+h1*inW+w1];\n    }\n}\n\n// Scatter Mean (accumulate)\n// NON-DETERMINISTIC across runs (issue #382); see scatter_mean_deterministic below.\nkernel void scatter_mean(\n    device const float* source [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const int* indices [[buffer(1)]],\n    device atomic_float* output [[buffer(2)]],\n    device atomic_int* counts [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& sourceSize [[buffer(4)]],\n    constant uint& featureSize [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant uint& outputSize [[buffer(6)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < sourceSize) {\n        uint row = gid / featureSize; uint col = gid % featureSize;\n        int targetRow = indices[row];\n        // An index outside [0, outputSize) contributes nowhere, as the deterministic variant skips it.\n        if (targetRow < 0 || (uint)targetRow >= outputSize) return;\n        atomic_fetch_add_explicit(&output[targetRow * featureSize + col], source[gid], memory_order_relaxed);\n        if (col == 0) atomic_fetch_add_explicit(&counts[targetRow], 1, memory_order_relaxed);\n    }\n}\n\n// scatter_mean — bit-deterministic variant (issue #382).\n// Parallelize per (dstRow, col) output cell; scan source rows in fixed\n// ascending order; no atomics.\nkernel void scatter_mean_deterministic(\n    device const float* source [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const int* indices [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(2)]],\n    device int* counts [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& sourceSize [[buffer(4)]],\n    constant uint& outputSize [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant uint& featureSize [[buffer(6)]],\n    uint2 gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    uint dstRow = gid.y;\n    uint col = gid.x;\n    if (dstRow >= outputSize || col >= featureSize) return;\n\n    uint numSrcRows = sourceSize / featureSize;\n    float sum = 0.0f;\n    int cnt = 0;\n    for (uint srcRow = 0u; srcRow < numSrcRows; srcRow++) {\n        if (uint(indices[srcRow]) == dstRow) {\n            sum += source[srcRow * featureSize + col];\n            if (col == 0u) cnt++;\n        }\n    }\n    output[dstRow * featureSize + col] = sum;\n    if (col == 0u) counts[dstRow] = cnt;\n}\n\n// Scatter Mean Divide\nkernel void scatter_mean_divide(\n    device float* output [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const int* counts [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& outputSize [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& featureSize [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < outputSize) {\n        uint row = gid / featureSize; int cnt = counts[row];\n        if (cnt > 0) output[gid] /= float(cnt);\n    }\n}\n\n// SELU backward\nkernel void selu_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        const float alpha = 1.6732632423543772f;\n        const float scale Differs only at x==0 and x==-0, where it is a factor-of-alpha error.\n        float grad = x >= 0.0f ? scale : scale * alpha * exp(x);\n        gradInput[gid] = gradOutput[gid] * grad;\n    }\n}\n\n// Hardsigmoid backward\nkernel void hardsigmoid_backward(\n    device const float* gradOutput [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* input [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* gradInput [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant uint& size [[buffer(3)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    if (gid < size) {\n        float x = input[gid];\n        float grad = (x > -3.0f && x < 3.0f) ? inHeight && wh >= 0 && wh < inWidth) v2 = inp[((b*inChannels+c)*inHeight+hl)*inWidth+wh];\n    if (hh >= 0 && hh < inHeight && wl >= 0 && wl < inWidth) v3 = thread per column element.\nkernel void unfold(\n    device const float* inp [[buffer(0)]], device float* outp [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& batch [[buffer(2)]], constant uint& channels [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& height [[buffer(4)]], constant uint& width [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant uint& kernelH [[buffer(6)]], constant uint& kernelW [[buffer(7)]],\n    constant uint& strideH [[buffer(8)]], constant uint& strideW [[buffer(9)]],\n    constant uint& padH [[buffer(10)]], constant uint& padW [[buffer(11)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    int outH = (int(height) + 2 * int(padH) - int(kernelH)) / int(strideH) + 1;\n    int outW = (int(width) + 2 * int(padW) - int(kernelW)) / int(strideW) + 1;\n    int colLen = outH * outW; int colCh = int(channels) * int(kernelH) * int(kernelW);\n    uint total = batch * uint(colCh) * uint(colLen);\n    if (gid >= total) return;\n    int idx = int(gid);\n    int b = idx / (colCh * colLen); int r = (int(kernelH) * int(kernelW));\n    int ki = k / int(kernelW); int kj = k % int(kernelW);\n    int ih = oh * int(strideH) + ki - int(padH); int iw = ow * int(strideW) + kj - int(padW);\n    float val val;\n}\n\n// Fold (Metal column layout, gather per output pixel — race-free inverse of unfold's scatter).\nkernel void fold(\n    device const float* inp [[buffer(0)]], device float* outp [[buffer(1)]],\n    constant uint& batch [[buffer(2)]], constant uint& channels [[buffer(3)]],\n    constant uint& outputH [[buffer(4)]], constant uint& outputW [[buffer(5)]],\n    constant uint& padW [[buffer(11)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    uint total = batch * channels * outputH * outputW;\n    if (gid >= total) return;\n    int idx = int(gid);\n    int iw = idx % unfoldW + ow];\n              }\n          }\n      }\n    outp[idx] = sum;\n}\n\n// ---- Locally connected conv2d (issue #646): gather-form, weights [outH,outW,outC,inC,kH,kW], no padding ----\nkernel void locally_connected_conv2d(\n    device const float* inp [[buffer(0)]], device const float* wgt [[buffer(1)]], device const float* bias [[buffer(2)]], device float* outp int inWidth) {\n    int hl = int(floor(h)); int wl = int(floor(w)); int hh = hl + 1; int wh = wl + 1;\n    float lh = h - float(hl); float lw = w - float(wl); float hhf = 1.0f - lh; float hwf = 1.0f - inHeight && wh >= 0 && wh < inWidth) v2 = inp[((b*inChannels+c)*inHeight+hl)*inWidth+wh];\n    if (hh >= 0 && hh < inHeight && wl >= 0 && wl < inWidth) v3 = inp[((b*inChannels+c)*inHeight+hh)*inWidth+wl];\n    if (hh >= 0 && hh < inHeight && wh >= 0 && wh < inWidth) v4 = inp[((b*inChannels+c)*inHeight+hh)*inWidth+wh];\n    return hhf*hwf*v1 + hhf*lw*v2 + ix++) {\n            float sx = x1 + pw * binW + (ix + 0.5f) * binW / rx;\n            acc += bilinear_sample_msl(input, planeBase, sy, sx, H, W);\n        }\n    }\n    output[gid] = acc / (ry * rx);\n}\n\nkernel void ps_roi_pool(\n    device const float* input [[buffer(0)]],\n    device const float* boxes [[buffer(1)]],\n    device float* output [[buffer(2)]],\n    constant int& N [[buffer(3)]], constant int& C [[buffer(4)]],\n    constant int& H [[buffer(5)]], constant int& W [[buffer(6)]],\n    constant int& K [[buffer(7)]], constant int& outH [[buffer(8)]],\n    constant int& outW [[buffer(9)]], constant int& outputChannels [[buffer(10)]],\n    constant float& spatialScale [[buffer(11)]],\n    uint gid [[thread_position_in_grid]])\n{\n    int total = K * outputChannels * outH * outW;\n    if ((int)gid >= total) return;\n    int pw = (int)gid % outW; return; }\n    float x1 = boxes[k * 5 + 1] * spatialScale;\n    float y1 = boxes[k * 5 + 2] * spatialScale;\n    float x2 = boxes[k * 5 + 3] * spatialScale;\n    float y2 = boxes[k * 5 + 4] * spatialScale;\n    float binH = max(y2 - y1, 0.1f) / outH;\n    float binW = max(x2 - x1, 0.1f) / outW;\n\n    int c = (co * outH + ph) * outW + pw;\n    if (c >= C) { output[gid] = 0.0; return; }\n    int planeBase = (n * C + c) * H * W;\n    int hs = (int)max(0.0f, floor(y1 + ph * binH));\n    int he = (int)min(float(H), ceil(y1 + (ph + 1) * binH));\n    int ws = (int)max(0.0f, floor(x1 + pw * binW));\n    int we = (int)min(float(W), ceil(x1 + (pw + 1) * binW));\n    float acc = 0; int cnt = 0;\n    for (int yy = hs; yy < he; yy++)\n        for (int xx = ws; xx < we; xx++) { acc += input[planeBase + yy * W + xx]; cnt++; }\n    output[gid] = cnt > 0 ? acc / cnt : 0.0;\n}\n" -> string!
